# Reproductive Week 2: Question-to-Learning Objective Mapping & Curriculum Overlap Reference

This document contains the canonical mapping of all 450 practice questions across the 15 Week 2 Reproductive modules to their official ScholarRx Brick Learning Objectives.

Use this file as the single source of truth for updating question metadata and cleaning up rationales in the repository.

---
